Generated by scripts/gen-licenses.ts from the installed runtime dependency closure — this package's "dependencies", resolved transitively through each dependency's own "dependencies" and "optionalDependencies" — never from all of node_modules, which also holds devDependency tooling that never ships. Regenerate after any dependency change: `bun run gen-licenses`. Do not hand-edit below this header.

------------------------------------------------

================================================
Vendored notices — mdview
================================================

Licence texts for code and tables ported or generated into the mdview binary from sources outside its Cargo closure; crates/mdview/notices/README.md records what each covers.

7 vendored notices.
